S﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌‌​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​‌‍﻿urat al-Jinn opens with a revelation that restructures the reader’s understanding of who the Qur’an addresses:
“﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​‌‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍﻿Say, ‘It has been revealed to me that a group of the jinn listened and said, “Indeed, we have heard

﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌​​‍﻿To the partner who wants to leave and believes they cannot:
T﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍﻿he franchise told you there is no exit. That is the franchise’s operational necessity, not a structural truth.

k﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌‌​​‌‍﻿ompromat. The ritual participation. The threat to family. The concealed-layer enforcement.
A﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​‌‍﻿ll of it is real. The paper does not minimize the visible-layer consequences of breaking the pact.
B﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​‌‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌​​‍﻿ut the structural truth is that the pact was never valid.

h﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌​​‍﻿as become Muslim – those have sought out the right course” (al-Jinn 72:14).
W﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍﻿ithin the framework, tawbah operates for you at the same structural level it operates for any created agent.
Y﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍﻿our fitrah – the original design your Creator gave you – is still there. Beneath the franchise-serving operations.

T﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​‌‍﻿he fitrah broadcasts. You can hear it. You have always heard it.
T﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​‌‌‍﻿he Creator’s offer is the same: turn back.

T﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌‌‍﻿o every created agent at every scale of creation. You are addressed.

﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌‌​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌‌​‌​‍﻿Chapter 29: The Witness’s Obligation and the Architecture of Hope
